## Logger Implementation & Correlation ID Library - Comprehensive Analysis

Based on thorough exploration of the recommendations-service repository, here's a complete breakdown of the logging and correlation ID implementation:

---

## 1. LOGGER IMPLEMENTATION

### Overview
The logger is **custom-built** on top of **NestJS Logger** (from `@nestjs/common`), wrapped in a custom implementation that integrates with **correlation-id-ts** library.

### Architecture: Custom Logger Class

**File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/configs/custom-logger.config.ts`

- **Class Name:** `RecoConsoleLogger`
- **Implements:** `LoggerService` from `@nestjs/common`
- **Type:** Dependency-injectable service (`@Injectable()`)
- **Features:**
  - Extends NestJS `ConsoleLogger` functionality
  - Integrates correlation IDs into every log output
  - JSON log formatting support
  - Customizable log levels (verbose, debug, log, warn, error, fatal)
  - Color-coded terminal output
  - Timestamp tracking and diff calculation
  - Process ID and context formatting

### Key Logger Methods
```typescript
- log(message, context?)          // Standard logging
- error(message, stack?, context?)  // Error logging with stack trace
- warn(message, context?)          // Warning logging
- debug(message, context?)         // Debug logging
- verbose(message, context?)       // Verbose logging
- fatal(message, context?)         // Fatal logging
- setLogLevels(levels)            // Configure log levels
- setContext(context)             // Set logging context
```

### Logger Integration Points

1. **HTTP Request Logger Middleware**
   - **File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/middlewares/logger.middleware.ts`
   - **Type:** Fastify plugin (using `fastify-plugin`)
   - **Function:** `fastifyHttpLogger`
   - **Features:**
     - Logs all HTTP requests/responses
     - Captures request/response headers, body, status code
     - Masks sensitive data (password, email, tenant, cookies, authorization headers)
     - Logs on 404 routes
     - Logs on errors
     - Different sensitivity based on environment (dev shows actual values, prod masks)

2. **Bootstrap Configuration**
   - **File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/main.ts`
   - Logger is instantiated with environment-specific settings
   - Configured with log levels, colors, compact mode, and JSON mode support

### NPM Package for Logger
- **Primary:** `@nestjs/common` (includes Logger abstract class)
- **No external logger library** - custom implementation
- **Peer Dependencies:** `newrelic` (for production monitoring)

---

## 2. CORRELATION ID IMPLEMENTATION

### Overview
Correlation ID is implemented using the **`correlation-id-ts`** npm package (v1.1.0) with custom middleware for request context management.

### Architecture & Components

#### A. Correlation ID Middleware
**File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/middlewares/correlation-id.middleware.ts`

```typescript
- Type: NestJS Middleware
- Implements: NestMiddleware
- Uses: correlation-id-ts library (correlator object)
```

**How it works:**
1. Reads `x-correlation-id` header from incoming request
2. If header missing, generates new UUID using Node's crypto module
3. Uses `correlator.withId()` from correlation-id-ts to establish context
4. Wraps request handling with correlation ID context

#### B. Request Store Utility
**File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/utils/request-store.util.ts`

- **Type:** AsyncLocalStorage-based request context manager
- **Purpose:** Stores request-scoped data (Rollbar options, custom fields)
- **Implementation:**
  - Uses Node's `AsyncLocalStorage` API
  - Thread-safe context management
  - Supports storing Rollbar configuration per request
  - Enables custom field merging and fingerprinting

#### C. Initialize Request Store Middleware
**File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/middlewares/initialize-request-store.middleware.ts`

- **Type:** Fastify middleware function
- **Purpose:** Initializes AsyncLocalStorage context for each request
- **Applied:** In `AppModule` for all routes via `consumer.apply(initializeRequestStoreMiddleware()).forRoutes('*path')`

### Correlation ID Header Configuration
**File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/constants/req.constants.ts`
```typescript
export const CORRELATION_ID_HEADER_NAME = 'x-correlation-id';
export const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 4000;
```

### NPM Package for Correlation ID
- **Package:** `correlation-id-ts` (v1.1.0)
- **Usage:** `import { correlator } from 'correlation-id-ts'`
- **Key Method:** `correlator.getId()` - retrieves current correlation ID
- **Key Method:** `correlator.withId({correlationId, fn})` - executes function within correlation context

---

## 3. INTEGRATION: HOW LOGGER & CORRELATION ID WORK TOGETHER

### Custom Logger Integration with Correlation ID
**Location:** `RecoConsoleLogger.printMessages()` method

```typescript
// Line 275 in custom-logger.config.ts
const correlationId = (correlator.getId() as string) || randomUUID();
```

**Every log output includes:**
- Correlation ID from the current context (via `correlator.getId()`)
- Fallback to new UUID if correlation ID not available
- Formatted into log output: `[Nest] PID - timestamp LEVEL [correlationId] [context] message`

### Rollbar Integration with Correlation ID
**File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/configs/rollbar.config.ts`

- **Transformer Function:** `transform()` function intercepts Rollbar events
- **Adds Correlation ID:** `data['custom']['correlationId'] = correlator.getId()`
- **Purpose:** Every error tracked to Rollbar includes the correlation ID for request tracing

---

## 4. EXECUTION TRACKING DECORATOR

### LogExecution Decorator
**File:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/decorators/log-execution.decorator.ts`

**Type:** Method decorator for comprehensive logging

**Features:**
- Logs method invocation with parameters
- Logs execution time with warnings if threshold exceeded
- Logs method response/result
- Logs errors with stack traces
- Integrates with Rollbar for error reporting
- Masks sensitive data (passwords, tokens, auth, cookies, etc.)
- Custom parameter and response formatters
- Configurable options (logParams, logResponse, logExecutionTime, logErrToRollbar, etc.)

**Usage Example:**
```typescript
@LogExecution({
  paramFormatter: (params) => ({ styleId: params.styleId }),
  logResponse: false,
  maskSensitiveData: true
})
async fetchFromThirdParty(data: Data): Promise<Result> { }
```

---

## 5. COMPLETE FILE STRUCTURE

### Core Logger & Correlation Files
```
src/
├── configs/
│   ├── custom-logger.config.ts          # Custom LoggerService implementation
│   └── rollbar.config.ts                # Rollbar integration with correlation ID
├── middlewares/
│   ├── logger.middleware.ts             # HTTP request/response logging
│   ├── correlation-id.middleware.ts     # Correlation ID extraction & context setup
│   ├── initialize-request-store.middleware.ts  # Request-scoped context
│   ├── bad-request-exception.filter.ts  # Exception handling
│   └── exceptions.filter.ts             # Global exception filter
├── decorators/
│   └── log-execution.decorator.ts       # Method execution logging decorator
├── interceptors/

├── modules/app/

    └── rollbar.integration-spec.ts
```

## 6. MIDDLEWARE EXECUTION ORDER

**In `main.ts` (bootstrap):**
1. **Fastify HTTP Logger Plugin** registered: `fastifyAdapter.getInstance().register(fastifyHttpLogger)`
2. **Correlation ID Middleware** applied as global middleware
3. **Exception Filters** registered: RollbarExceptionFilter, BadRequestExceptionFilter
4. **Global Pipes** registered: ValidationPipe

**In `AppModule.configure()`:**
3. **Request Store Initialization Middleware** applied to all routes

---

| Package | Version | Purpose |
|---------|---------|---------|
| `correlation-id-ts` | ^1.1.0 | Correlation ID management and context tracking |
| `@nestjs/common` | ^11.1.13 | Logger interface and NestJS utilities |
| `newrelic` | ^12.25.0 | Application performance monitoring |
| `rollbar` | ^2.26.5 | Error tracking and monitoring |
| `fastify-plugin` | (implicit) | For HTTP logger plugin functionality |

---

## 8. KEY ENVIRONMENTAL VARIABLES

**Logging Configuration:**
- `IS_COMPACT_LOGS` - Compact log formatting
- `IS_JSON_LIKE_LOGS` - JSON output format
- `EXECUTION_TIME_THRESHOLD_MS` - Threshold for long execution warnings

**Monitoring:**
- `ROLLBAR_ACCESS_TOKEN` - Rollbar authentication
- `NODE_ENV` - Environment (development, staging, production)
- `SERVER_NAME` - Server identifier
- `PORT` - Application port

---

## 9. DOCUMENTATION REFERENCE

**CLAUDE.md Section:** Lines 344-429

Key guidelines:
- **LogExecution** decorator should wrap all important methods and 3rd party requests
- Custom logger uses `RecoConsoleLogger` for dependency injection
- All logs automatically include correlation IDs
- Sensitive data is masked in logs based on environment
- Both NestJS `Logger` and custom `RecoConsoleLogger` are available for use

---

## Summary

The recommendations-service implements a **sophisticated, production-grade logging and request tracking system**:

1. **Logger:** Custom `RecoConsoleLogger` class wrapping NestJS Logger with correlation ID integration
2. **Correlation ID:** `correlation-id-ts` library (v1.1.0) for context-aware request tracking
3. **Middleware Chain:** HTTP logging → Correlation ID → Request Store initialization
4. **Monitoring:** Integrated with New Relic (APM) and Rollbar (error tracking)
5. **Decorator Pattern:** `@LogExecution` provides comprehensive method-level logging with sensitive data masking
6. **Architecture:** Fully typed, injectable, configurable per environment

14	const LOG_LEVEL_VALUES: Record<LogLevel, number> = {
15		verbose: 0,
16		debug: 1,
17		log: 2,
18		warn: 3,
19		error: 4,
20		fatal: 5,

23	export function isLogLevelEnabled(
24		targetLevel: LogLevel,
25		logLevels: LogLevel[] | undefined,
26	): boolean {
27		if (!logLevels || (Array.isArray(logLevels) && logLevels?.length === 0)) {
28			return false;
29		}
30		if (logLevels.includes(targetLevel)) {
31			return true;
32		}
33		const highestLogLevelValue = logLevels
34			.map((level) => LOG_LEVEL_VALUES[level])
35			.sort((a, b) => b - a)?.[0];
36	
37		const targetLevelValue = LOG_LEVEL_VALUES[targetLevel];
38		return targetLevelValue >= highestLogLevelValue!;
39	}

41	export const isUndefined = (obj: any): obj is undefined => typeof obj === 'undefined';
42	
43	export const isObject = (fn: any): fn is object => !isNil(fn) && typeof fn === 'object';
44	
45	export const isPlainObject = (fn: any): fn is object => {
46		if (!isObject(fn)) {
47			return false;
48		}
49		const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(fn);
50		if (proto === null) {
51			return true;
52		}
53		const ctor =
54			Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(proto, 'constructor') && proto.constructor;
55		return (
56			typeof ctor === 'function' &&
57			ctor instanceof ctor &&
58			Function.prototype.toString.call(ctor) === Function.prototype.toString.call(Object)
59		);
60	};

227			this.printMessages(messages, context, 'verbose');
228		}
229	
230		fatal(message: any, context?: string): void;
231		fatal(message: any, ...optionalParams: [...any, string?]): void;
232		fatal(message: any, ...optionalParams: any[]) {
233			if (!this.isLevelEnabled('fatal')) {
234				return;
235			}
236			const { messages, context } = this.getContextAndMessagesToPrint([
237				message,
238				...optionalParams,
239			]);
240			this.printMessages(messages, context, 'fatal');
241		}
242	
243		setLogLevels(levels: LogLevel[]) {
244			if (!this.options) {
245				this.options = {};
246			}
247			this.options.logLevels = levels;
248		}
249	
250		setContext(context: string) {
251			this.context = context;
252		}
253	
254		resetContext() {
255			// @ts-ignore
256			this.context = this.originalContext;
257		}
258	
259		isLevelEnabled(level: LogLevel): boolean {
260			const logLevels = this.options?.logLevels;
261			return isLogLevelEnabled(level, logLevels);
262		}
263	
264		protected getTimestamp(): string {
265			return dateTimeFormatter.format(Date.now());
266		}
267	
268		protected printMessages(
269			messages: unknown[],
270			context = '',
271			logLevel: LogLevel = 'log',
272			writeStreamType?: 'stdout' | 'stderr',
273			errorStack?: unknown,
274		) {
275			const correlationId = (correlator.getId() as string) || randomUUID();
276	
277			messages.forEach((message) => {
278				if (this.options.json) {
279					// @ts-ignore
280					this.printAsJson(message, correlationId, {
281						context,
282						logLevel,
283						writeStreamType,
284						errorStack,
285					});
286					return;
287				}
288				const pidMessage = this.formatPid(process.pid);
289				const contextMessage = this.formatContext(context);
290				const timestampDiff = this.updateAndGetTimestampDiff();
291				const formattedLogLevel = logLevel.toUpperCase().padStart(7, ' ');
292				const formattedMessage = this.formatMessage(
293					logLevel,
294					message,
295					correlationId,
296					pidMessage,
297					formattedLogLevel,
298					contextMessage,
299					timestampDiff,
300				);
301	
302				process[writeStreamType ?? 'stdout'].write(formattedMessage);
303			});
304		}
305	
306		protected printAsJson(
307			message: unknown,
308			correlationId: string,
309			options: {
310				context: string;
311				logLevel: LogLevel;
312				writeStreamType?: 'stdout' | 'stderr';
313				errorStack?: unknown;
314			},
315		) {
316			type JsonLogObject = {
317				level: LogLevel;
318				pid: number;
319				timestamp: number;
320				correlationId: string;
321				message: unknown;
322				context?: string;
323				stack?: unknown;
324			};
325	
326			const logObject: JsonLogObject = {
327				level: options.logLevel,
328				pid: process.pid,
329				timestamp: Date.now(),
330				correlationId,
331				message,
332			};
333	
334			if (options.context) {
335				logObject.context = options.context;
336			}
337	
338			if (options.errorStack) {
339				logObject.stack = options.errorStack;
340			}
341	
342			const formattedMessage =
343				!this.options.colors && this.inspectOptions.compact === true
344					? JSON.stringify(logObject, this.stringifyReplacer)
345					: inspect(logObject, this.inspectOptions);
346			process[options.writeStreamType ?? 'stdout'].write(`${formattedMessage}\n`);
347		}
348	
349		protected formatPid(pid: number) {
350			return `[${this.options.prefix}] ${pid}  - `;
351		}
352	
353		protected formatContext(context: string): string {
354			if (!context) {
355				return '';
356			}
357	
358			context = `[${context}] `;
359			return this.options.colors ? yellow(context) : context;
360		}
361	
362		protected formatMessage(
363			logLevel: LogLevel,
364			message: unknown,
365			correlationId: string,
366			pidMessage: string,
367			formattedLogLevel: string,
368			contextMessage: string,
369			timestampDiff: string,
370		) {
371			const output = this.stringifyMessage(message, logLevel);
372			pidMessage = this.colorize(pidMessage, logLevel);
373			formattedLogLevel = this.colorize(formattedLogLevel, logLevel);
374			return `${pidMessage}${this.getTimestamp()} ${formattedLogLevel} ${correlationId}${contextMessage}${output}${timestampDiff}\n`;
375		}
376	
377		protected stringifyMessage(message: unknown, logLevel: LogLevel): string {
378			if (isFunction(message)) {
379				const messageAsStr = Function.prototype.toString.call(message);
380				const isClass = messageAsStr.startsWith('class ');
381				if (isClass) {
382					// @ts-ignore
383					return this.stringifyMessage(message.name, logLevel);
384				}
385				// @ts-ignore
386				return this.stringifyMessage(message(), logLevel);
387			}
388	
389			if (typeof message === 'string') {
390				return this.colorize(message, logLevel);
391			}
392	
393			const outputText = inspect(message, this.inspectOptions);
394			if (isPlainObject(message)) {
395				return `Object(${Object.keys(message).length}) ${outputText}`;
396			}
397			if (Array.isArray(message)) {
398				return `Array(${message.length}) ${outputText}`;
399			}
400			return outputText;
401		}
402	
403		protected colorize(message: string, logLevel: LogLevel) {
404			if (!this.options.colors || this.options.json) {
405				return message;
406			}
407			const color = this.getColorByLogLevel(logLevel);
408			return color(message);
409		}
410	
411		protected printStackTrace(stack: string) {
412			if (!stack || this.options.json) {
413				return;
414			}
415			process.stderr.write(`${stack}\n`);
416		}
417	
418		protected updateAndGetTimestampDiff(): string {
419			// @ts-ignore
420			const includeTimestamp = ConsoleLogger.lastTimestampAt && this.options?.timestamp;
421			const result = includeTimestamp
422				? // @ts-ignore
423					this.formatTimestampDiff(Date.now() - ConsoleLogger.lastTimestampAt!)
424				: '';
425			// @ts-ignore
426			ConsoleLogger.lastTimestampAt = Date.now();
427			return result;
428		}
429	
430		protected formatTimestampDiff(timestampDiff: number) {
431			const formattedDiff = ` +${timestampDiff}ms`;
432			return this.options.colors ? yellow(formattedDiff) : formattedDiff;
433		}
434	
435		protected getInspectOptions() {
436			let breakLength = this.options.breakLength;
437			if (typeof breakLength === 'undefined') {
438				breakLength = this.options.colors
439					? this.options.compact
440						? Infinity
441						: undefined
442					: this.options.compact === false
443						? undefined
444						: Infinity;
445			}
446	
447			const inspectOptions: InspectOptions = {
448				depth: this.options.depth ?? DEFAULT_DEPTH,
449				sorted: this.options.sorted,
450				showHidden: this.options.showHidden,
451				compact: this.options.compact ?? (this.options.json ? true : false),
452				colors: this.options.colors,
453				breakLength,
454			};
455	
456			if (this.options.maxArrayLength) {
457				inspectOptions.maxArrayLength = this.options.maxArrayLength;
458			}
459			if (this.options.maxStringLength) {
460				inspectOptions.maxStringLength = this.options.maxStringLength;
461			}
462	
463			return inspectOptions;
464		}
465	
466		protected stringifyReplacer(_: string, value: unknown) {
467			if (typeof value === 'bigint') {
468				return value.toString();
469			}
470			if (typeof value === 'symbol') {
471				return value.toString();
472			}
473	
474			if (value instanceof Map || value instanceof Set || value instanceof Error) {
475				return `${inspect(value, this.inspectOptions)}`;
476			}
477			return value;
478		}
479	
480		private getContextAndMessagesToPrint(args: unknown[]) {
481			if (args?.length <= 1) {
482				return { messages: args, context: this.context };
483			}
484			const lastElement = args[args.length - 1];
485			const isContext = isString(lastElement);
486			if (!isContext) {
487				return { messages: args, context: this.context };
488			}
489			return {
490				context: lastElement,
491				messages: args.slice(0, args.length - 1),
492			};
493		}
494	
495		private getContextAndStackAndMessagesToPrint(args: unknown[]) {
496			if (args.length === 2) {
497				return this.isStackFormat(args[1])
498					? {
499							messages: [args[0]],
500							stack: args[1] as string,
501							context: this.context,
502						}
503					: {
504							messages: [args[0]],
505							context: args[1] as string,
506						};
507			}
508	
509			const { messages, context } = this.getContextAndMessagesToPrint(args);
510			if (messages?.length <= 1) {
511				return { messages, context };
512			}
513			const lastElement = messages[messages.length - 1];
514			const isStack = isString(lastElement);
515			if (!isStack && !isUndefined(lastElement)) {
516				return { messages, context };
517			}
518			return {
519				stack: lastElement,
520				messages: messages.slice(0, messages.length - 1),
521				context,
522			};
523		}
524	
525		private isStackFormat(stack: unknown) {
526			if (!isString(stack) && !isUndefined(stack)) {
527				return false;
528			}
529	
530			return /^(.)+\n\s+at .+:\d+:\d+/.test(stack!);
531		}
532	
533		private getColorByLogLevel(level: LogLevel) {
534			switch (level) {
535				case 'debug':
536					return clc.magentaBright;
537				case 'warn':
538					return clc.yellow;
539				case 'error':
540					return clc.red;
541				case 'verbose':
542					return clc.cyanBright;
543				case 'fatal':
544					return clc.bold;
545				default:
546					return clc.green;
547			}
548		}
549	}
550

1	import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
2	
3	import { Injectable, NestMiddleware } from '@nestjs/common';
4	import { correlator } from 'correlation-id-ts';
5	import { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';
6	
7	import { CORRELATION_ID_HEADER_NAME } from '@constants/req.constants';
8	
9	@Injectable()
10	export class CorrelationIdMiddleware implements NestMiddleware {
11		use(req: FastifyRequest['raw'], _: FastifyReply['raw'], next: () => void): void {
12			const correlationIdFromHeader = req.headers[CORRELATION_ID_HEADER_NAME] as string;
13	
14			const id = correlationIdFromHeader || randomUUID();
15	
16			correlator.withId({
17				correlationId: id,
18				fn: () => {
19					next();
20				},
21			});
22		}
23	}
24

1	import { IncomingHttpHeaders } from 'node:http2';
2	
3	import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
4	import { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply, FastifyPluginCallback } from 'fastify';
5	import { HttpHeader } from 'fastify/types/utils';
6	import fp from 'fastify-plugin';
7	
8	import { isDevEnv } from '@utils/common.util';
9	
10	const FIELDS_WITH_SENSITIVE_DATA = [
11		'req.body.password',
12		'req.body.email',
13		'req.body.tenant',
14		'req.body.recipient',
15		'req.headers.cookie',
16		'req.headers.authorization',
17		'req.headers.proxyAuthorization',
18		'res.headers.cookie',
19		'res.headers["set-cookie"]',
20		'res.headers.authorization',
21		'res.headers.proxyAuthorization',
22	];
23	
24	export const logHttpRequest = (
25		req: FastifyRequest,
26		res: FastifyReply,
27		error?: Error,
28	): void => {
29		const logger = new Logger('HTTP Request');
30		const body = req.body ?? {};
31		const requestHeaders = req.headers;
32		const method = req.method;
33		const url = req.url;
34		const originalUrl = req.originalUrl;
35		const responseHeaders = res.getHeaders();
36		const statusCode = res.statusCode;
37		const ips = req.ips;
38		const ip = req.ip;
39	
40		const validateHeaders = (
41			headers:
42				| IncomingHttpHeaders
43				| Record<HttpHeader, string | number | string[] | undefined>,
44		): Record<string, string> =>
45			Object.entries(headers).reduce<Record<string, string>>((acc, [key, value]) => {
46				if (FIELDS_WITH_SENSITIVE_DATA.includes(`req.headers.${key}`)) {
47					acc[key] = isDevEnv() ? JSON.stringify(value) : '*****';
48				} else {
49					acc[key] = JSON.stringify(value);
50				}
51				return acc;
52			}, {});
53	
54		const validatedBody = Object.entries(body).reduce<Record<string, string>>(
55			(acc, [key, value]) => {
56				if (FIELDS_WITH_SENSITIVE_DATA.includes(`req.body.${key}`)) {
57					acc[key] = isDevEnv() ? JSON.stringify(value) : '*****';
58				} else {
59					acc[key] = JSON.stringify(value);
60				}
61				return acc;
62			},
63			{},
64		);
65	
66		const isErrorStatus = statusCode >= 400;
67		const logMethod = isErrorStatus || error ? 'error' : 'log';
68	
69		const logData: Record<string, unknown> = {
70			req: {
71				method,
72				url,
73				originalUrl,
74				ips,
75				ip,
76				headers: validateHeaders(requestHeaders),
77				body: validatedBody,
78			},
79			res: { statusCode, headers: validateHeaders(responseHeaders) },
80		};
81	
82		if (error) {
83			logData['error'] = {
84				message: error.message,
85				name: error.name,
86				stack: error.stack,
87			};
88		}
89	
90		logger[logMethod](logData);
91	};
92	
93	const httpLoggerPlugin: FastifyPluginCallback = (fastify, _options, done) => {
94		fastify.addHook('onResponse', (request, reply, done) => {
95			if (reply.statusCode === 404) {
96				const notFoundError = new Error(
97					`Route not found: ${request.method} ${request.url}`,
98				);
99				notFoundError.name = 'NotFoundError';
100				logHttpRequest(request, reply, notFoundError);
101			} else {
102				logHttpRequest(request, reply);
103			}
104			done();
105		});
106	
107		fastify.addHook('onError', (request, reply, error, done) => {
108			logHttpRequest(request, reply, error);
109			done();
110		});
111	
112		done();
113	};
114	
115	export const fastifyHttpLogger = fp(httpLoggerPlugin, {
116		name: 'fastify-http-logger',
117	});
118

1	/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-unnecessary-condition */
2	import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'async_hooks';
3	
4	import { ROLLBAR } from '@constants/common.constant';
5	
6	export interface RollbarOptions {
7		/**
8		 * Custom fields to be merged with other event-specific fields.
9		 * These fields are searchable in the Rollbar dashboard.
10		 *
11		 * @example
12		 * fields: { accountId: 123, clientName: 'accounts-ui' }
13		 */
14		fields?: Record<string, number | string | boolean>;
15	
16		/**
17		 * Custom data to be merged with other event-specific data.
18		 * This can include any additional context or metadata for the event.
19		 *
20		 * @example
21		 * data: { sessionId: "abc123", failedConstraints: { email: ['email must be an email'] } }
22		 */
23		data?: Record<string, unknown>;
24	
25		/**
26		 * A custom string to be added to Rollbar's default fingerprint.
27		 * This is useful for resolving issues with over-grouping of events.
28		 *
29		 * @example
30		 * fingerprintContext: "custom-fingerprint"
31		 */
32		fingerprintContext?: string;
33	
34		/**
35		 * A custom title for the issue, which will appear in the Rollbar dashboard.
36		 *
37		 * @example
38		 * issueTitle: "Unexpected Error in Payment Processing"
39		 */
40		issueTitle?: string;
41	
42		/**
43		 * A list of entity names. If a NotFoundError is caused by any of these entities,
44		 * the corresponding Rollbar event will be ignored and not logged.
45		 *
46		 * @example
47		 * notFoundErrorsIgnoredEntities: ["User", "Order"]
48		 */
49		notFoundErrorsIgnoredEntities?: string[];
50	}
51	
52	export interface RequestStore {
53		rollbar: RollbarOptions | undefined;
54		[key: string]: unknown;
55	}
56	
57	const defaultStoreValue: RequestStore = {
58		[ROLLBAR]: undefined,
59	};
60	
61	export function deepClone<T>(obj: T): T {
62		if (obj === null || obj === undefined || typeof obj !== 'object') return obj;
63	
64		const str = JSON.stringify(obj);
65		return JSON.parse(str) as T;
66	}
67	
68	export function mergeObjectsDeeply<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
69		target: T,
70		source: T,
71	): T {
72		if (!target || typeof target !== 'object') {
73			return source;
74		}
75	
76		if (!source || typeof source !== 'object') {
77			return target;
78		}
79	
80		for (const key in source) {
81			if (source[key] && typeof source[key] === 'object') {
82				target[key] = mergeObjectsDeeply(target[key] || {}, source[key]) as T[Extract<
83					keyof T,
84					string
85				>];
86			} else {
87				target[key] = source[key];
88			}
89		}
90		return target;
91	}
92	
93	export const requestStore = {
94		asyncLocalStorage: new AsyncLocalStorage<RequestStore>(),
95	
96		set<T extends keyof RequestStore>(key: T, value: RequestStore[T]): void {
97			const currentStore = this.asyncLocalStorage.getStore();
98			if (currentStore) {
99				currentStore[key] = value;
100			} else {
101				this.asyncLocalStorage.enterWith({ ...defaultStoreValue, [key]: value });
102			}
103		},
104	
105		getOrThrow<T extends keyof RequestStore>(key: T): RequestStore[T] {
106			const currentStore = this.getStoreOrThrow();
107			const value = currentStore[key];
108			if (!value) {
109				throw new Error(`Store value for key ${key} is not set`);
110			}
111			return value;
112		},
113	
114		get<T extends keyof RequestStore>(key: T): RequestStore[T] {
115			const currentStore = this.asyncLocalStorage.getStore();
116			return currentStore?.[key];
117		},
118	
119		update<T extends keyof RequestStore>(key: T, updates: Partial<RequestStore[T]>): void {
120			if (key !== ROLLBAR) {
121				throw new Error(`Key ${key} is not supported`);
122			}
123			const currentRollbar = deepClone(this.get(key)) ?? ({} as RollbarOptions);
124			const updatedRollbar = mergeObjectsDeeply(updates, currentRollbar) as RollbarOptions;
125			this.set('rollbar', updatedRollbar);
126		},
127	
128		getStoreOrThrow(): RequestStore {
129			const currentStore = this.asyncLocalStorage.getStore();
130			if (!currentStore) {
131				throw new Error('Store is not initialized');
132			}
133			return currentStore;
134		},
135	
136		initializeStore(initStoreValues: Partial<RequestStore> = {}): void {
137			this.asyncLocalStorage.enterWith({ ...defaultStoreValue, ...initStoreValues });
138		},
139	
140		run(store: RequestStore, callback: () => void): void {
141			this.asyncLocalStorage.run(store, callback);
142		},
143	};
144

1	import { performance } from 'perf_hooks';
2	
3	import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
4	import * as Rollbar from 'rollbar';
5	
6	import { rollbar } from '@config/rollbar.config';
7	
8	import { EXECUTION_TIME_THRESHOLD_MS } from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
9	
10	export interface LogExecutionOptions<TParams, TResponse> {
11		logParams?: boolean;
12		logResponse?: boolean;
13		logExecutionTime?: boolean;
14		logErrToRollbar?: boolean;
15		logLongExecution?: boolean;
16		rollbarLevel?: 'debug' | 'info' | 'warning' | 'error';
17		maskSensitiveData?: boolean;
18		sensitiveKeys?: string[];
19		customContext?: Record<string, unknown>;
20		paramFormatter?: (params: TParams) => unknown;
21		responseFormatter?: (response: TResponse) => unknown;
22	}
23	
24	const DEFAULT_OPTIONS: LogExecutionOptions<unknown, unknown> = {
25		logParams: true,
26		logResponse: true,
27		logExecutionTime: true,
28		logErrToRollbar: true,
29		logLongExecution: true,
30		rollbarLevel: 'debug',
31		maskSensitiveData: true,
32		sensitiveKeys: [
33			'password',
34			'pass',
35			'token',
36			'secret',
37			'auth',
38			'authorization',
39			'cookie',
40			'session',
41			'credentials',
42			'apiKey',
43			'_cookie',
44			'csrf',
45			'_csrf',
46		],
47		paramFormatter: <TParams>(params: TParams) => params,
48		responseFormatter: <TResponse>(response: TResponse) => response,
49	};
50	
51	const thresholdMs = Number(process.env[EXECUTION_TIME_THRESHOLD_MS]);
52	
53	export function LogExecution<TParams, TResponse>(
54		options: LogExecutionOptions<TParams, TResponse> = {},
55	): MethodDecorator {
56		const config: LogExecutionOptions<TParams, TResponse> = {
57			...DEFAULT_OPTIONS,
58			...options,
59		};
60	
61		return function (
62			target: unknown,
63			propertyKey: string | symbol,
64			descriptor: PropertyDescriptor,
65		) {
66			const originalMethod = descriptor.value as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown;
67			const className = (target as { constructor: { name: string } }).constructor.name;
68			const methodName = String(propertyKey);
69			const logger = new Logger(`${className}.${methodName}`);
70	
71			descriptor.value = async function (...args: unknown[]) {
72				const startTime = performance.now();
73	
74				try {
75					const logContext = {
76						className,
77						methodName,
78						timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
79						...(config.customContext ?? {}),
80					};
81	
82					let paramsToLog: unknown = config.paramFormatter
83						? config.paramFormatter(args as TParams)
84						: args;
85					if (config.maskSensitiveData) {
86						paramsToLog = maskSensitiveData(paramsToLog, config.sensitiveKeys);
87					}
88	
89					if (config.logParams) {
90						logger.log(
91							`[LogExecution]: Method invoked with parameters, ${JSON.stringify({
92								params: paramsToLog,
93								...logContext,
94							})}`,
95						);
96					}
97	
98					const result = await originalMethod.apply(this, args);
99					const endTime = performance.now();
100					const executionTime = endTime - startTime;
101	
102					let responseToLog: unknown = config.responseFormatter
103						? config.responseFormatter(result as TResponse)
104						: result;
105					if (config.maskSensitiveData) {
106						responseToLog = maskSensitiveData(responseToLog, config.sensitiveKeys);
107					}
108	
109					const successLogContext = {
110						...logContext,
111						executionTimeMs: executionTime,
112						response: config.logResponse ? responseToLog : undefined,
113					};
114	
115					if (config.logExecutionTime) {
116						logger.log(
117							`[LogExecution]:Method completed in ${executionTime.toFixed(2)}ms. Result: ${JSON.stringify(successLogContext)}`,
118						);
119					}
120	
121					if (executionTime > thresholdMs) {
122						logger.warn(
123							`[LogExecution]: Long execution ${executionTime.toFixed(
124								2,
125							)}ms, logContext: ${JSON.stringify({ ...successLogContext, success: true })}`,
126						);
127	
128						if (config.logLongExecution) {
129							try {
130								const rollbar = getRollbarInstance();
131								if (rollbar) {
132									rollbar.warning(
133										`[LogExecution]: Long execution ${executionTime.toFixed(2)}ms.`,
134										{ ...successLogContext, success: true },
135									);
136								}
137							} catch {}
138						}
139					}
140	
141					return result;
142				} catch (error) {
143					const endTime = performance.now();
144					const executionTime = endTime - startTime;
145					const errorLogContext = {
146						className,
147						methodName,
148						timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
149						executionTimeMs: executionTime,
150						error: {
151							message: (error as Error).message,
152							stack: (error as Error).stack,
153							name: (error as Error).name,
154						},
155						...(config.customContext ?? {}),
156					};
157	
158					logger.error(
159						`[LogExecution]: Method failed after ${executionTime.toFixed(2)}ms: ${
160							(error as Error).message
161						}, errorLogContext: ${JSON.stringify(errorLogContext)}`,
162					);
163	
164					if (config.logErrToRollbar) {
165						try {
166							const rollbar = getRollbarInstance();
167							if (rollbar) {
168								rollbar.error(`Method ${className}.${methodName} failed.`, error as Error, {
169									...errorLogContext,
170									success: false,
171								});
172							}
173						} catch {}
174					}
175	
176					throw error;
177				}
178			};
179	
180			return descriptor;
181		};
182	}
183	
184	function maskSensitiveData(data: unknown, sensitiveKeys: string[] = []): unknown {
185		if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
186			return data;
187		}
188	
189		if (Array.isArray(data)) {
190			return data.map((item) => maskSensitiveData(item, sensitiveKeys));
191		}
192	
193		const masked = { ...data } as Record<string, unknown>;
194	
195		for (const key in masked) {
196			if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(masked, key)) {
197				const lowerKey = key.toLowerCase();
198	
199				if (
200					sensitiveKeys.some((sensitiveKey) =>
201						lowerKey.includes(sensitiveKey.toLowerCase()),
202					)
203				) {
204					masked[key] = '***MASKED***';
205				} else if (typeof masked[key] === 'object' && masked[key] !== null) {
206					masked[key] = maskSensitiveData(masked[key], sensitiveKeys);
207				}
208			}
209		}
210	
211		return masked;
212	}
213	
214	function getRollbarInstance(): Rollbar | null {
215		return rollbar;
216	}
217	
```

1	import { FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';
2	
3	import { requestStore } from '@utils/request-store.util';
4	
5	export function initializeRequestStoreMiddleware(): (
6		req: FastifyRequest['raw'],
7		res: FastifyReply['raw'],
8		next: () => void,
9	) => void {
10		return (req: FastifyRequest['raw'], _res: FastifyReply['raw'], next: () => void) => {
11			requestStore.initializeStore({ request: req });
12			next();
13		};
14	}

1	import { performance } from 'perf_hooks';
2	
3	import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
4	
5	const EXECUTION_TIME_THRESHOLD_MS = 3000;
6	
7	export interface LogExecutionOptions<TParams = unknown, TResponse = unknown> {
8		logParams?: boolean;
9		logResponse?: boolean;
10		logExecutionTime?: boolean;
11		maskSensitiveData?: boolean;
12		sensitiveKeys?: string[];
13		customContext?: Record<string, unknown>;
14		paramFormatter?: (params: TParams) => unknown;
15		responseFormatter?: (response: TResponse) => unknown;
16	}
17	
18	const DEFAULT_SENSITIVE_KEYS: string[] = [
19		'password',
20		'pass',
21		'token',
22		'secret',
23		'auth',
24		'authorization',
25		'cookie',
26		'session',
27		'credentials',
28		'apiKey',
29		'csrf',
30		'refreshToken',
31		'accessToken',
32	];
33	
34	const DEFAULT_OPTIONS: LogExecutionOptions = {
35		logParams: true,
36		logResponse: true,
37		logExecutionTime: true,
38		maskSensitiveData: true,
39		sensitiveKeys: DEFAULT_SENSITIVE_KEYS,
40	};
41	
42	const REQUEST_LIKE_KEYS = new Set([
43		'raw',
44		'socket',
45		'headers',
46		'hostname',
47		'ip',
48		'protocol',
49		'routeOptions',
50		'routerPath',
51	]);
52	
53	function isRequestObject(value: unknown): boolean {
54		if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return false;
55		const keys = Object.keys(value as Record<string, unknown>);
56		return REQUEST_LIKE_KEYS.has(keys[0] ?? '') && keys.length > 5;
57	}
58	
59	function isResponseObject(value: unknown): boolean {
60		if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return false;
61		const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;
62		return typeof obj['send'] === 'function' && typeof obj['status'] === 'function';
63	}
64	
65	function extractRequestInfo(req: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
66		return {
67			method: req['method'],
68			url: req['url'],
69			ip: req['ip'],
70			userAgent: (req['headers'] as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.['user-agent'],
71		};
72	}
73	
74	function sanitizeArgs(args: unknown[]): unknown[] {
75		return args.map((arg) => {
76			if (isRequestObject(arg)) return extractRequestInfo(arg as Record<string, unknown>);
77			if (isResponseObject(arg)) return '[FastifyReply]';
78			return arg;
79		});
80	}
81	
82	export function maskSensitiveData(
83		data: unknown,
84		sensitiveKeys: string[] = DEFAULT_SENSITIVE_KEYS,
85	): unknown {
86		if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return data;
87	
88		if (Array.isArray(data)) {
89			return data.map((item) => maskSensitiveData(item, sensitiveKeys));
90		}
91	
92		const masked = { ...data } as Record<string, unknown>;
93	
94		for (const key in masked) {
95			if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(masked, key)) continue;
96	
97			const lowerKey = key.toLowerCase();
98	
99			if (sensitiveKeys.some((sk) => lowerKey.includes(sk.toLowerCase()))) {
100				masked[key] = '***MASKED***';
101			} else if (typeof masked[key] === 'object' && masked[key] !== null) {
102				masked[key] = maskSensitiveData(masked[key], sensitiveKeys);
103			}
104		}
105	
106		return masked;
107	}
108	
109	export function LogExecution<TParams = unknown, TResponse = unknown>(
110		options: LogExecutionOptions<TParams, TResponse> = {},
111	): MethodDecorator {
112		const config = { ...DEFAULT_OPTIONS, ...options };
113	
114		return function (
115			target: unknown,
116			propertyKey: string | symbol,
117			descriptor: PropertyDescriptor,
118		): PropertyDescriptor {
119			const originalMethod = descriptor.value as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown;
120			const className = (target as { constructor: { name: string } }).constructor.name;
121			const methodName = String(propertyKey);
122			const logger = new Logger(`${className}.${methodName}`);
123	
124			descriptor.value = async function (...args: unknown[]) {
125				const startTime = performance.now();
126	
127				try {
128					const logContext = {
129						className,
130						methodName,
131						timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
132						...(config.customContext ?? {}),
133					};
134	
135					if (config.logParams) {
136						let paramsToLog: unknown = config.paramFormatter
137							? config.paramFormatter(args as TParams)
138							: sanitizeArgs(args);
139	
140						if (config.maskSensitiveData) {
141							paramsToLog = maskSensitiveData(paramsToLog, config.sensitiveKeys);
142						}
143	
144						logger.log(
145							`Invoked | ${JSON.stringify({ params: paramsToLog, ...logContext })}`,
146						);
147					}
148	
149					const result = await originalMethod.apply(this, args);
150					const executionTime = performance.now() - startTime;
151	
152					if (config.logExecutionTime) {
153						let responseToLog: unknown = config.responseFormatter
154							? config.responseFormatter(result as TResponse)
155							: result;
156	
157						if (config.maskSensitiveData) {
158							responseToLog = maskSensitiveData(responseToLog, config.sensitiveKeys);
159						}
160	
161						const successContext = {
162							...logContext,
163							executionTimeMs: Math.round(executionTime * 100) / 100,
164							...(config.logResponse ? { response: responseToLog } : {}),
165						};
166	
167						logger.log(
168							`Completed in ${executionTime.toFixed(2)}ms | ${JSON.stringify(successContext)}`,
169						);
170					}
171	
172					if (executionTime > EXECUTION_TIME_THRESHOLD_MS) {
173						logger.warn(
174							`Slow execution: ${executionTime.toFixed(2)}ms | ${className}.${methodName}`,
175						);
176					}
177	
178					return result;
179				} catch (error) {
180					const executionTime = performance.now() - startTime;
181	
182					logger.error(
183						`Failed after ${executionTime.toFixed(2)}ms: ${(error as Error).message}`,
184						(error as Error).stack,
185					);
186	
187					throw error;
188				}
189			};
190	
191			return descriptor;
192		};
193	}
194

1	import fastifyCookie from '@fastify/cookie';
2	import { Logger, ValidationPipe } from '@nestjs/common';
3	import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
4	import { FastifyAdapter, NestFastifyApplication } from '@nestjs/platform-fastify';
5	
6	import { AppModule } from '@modules/app/app.module';
7	
8	import { getEnv } from '@utils/get-env-variable.util';
9	import { setupSwagger } from '@utils/setup-swagger.util';
10	
11	import {
12		FRONTEND_URL,
13		GLOBAL_PREFIX,
14		NODE_ENV,
15		PORT,
16	} from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
17	
18	import { Environment } from '@interfaces/env-variables.interface';
19	
20	declare const module: {
21		hot?: { accept: () => void; dispose: (callback: () => void) => void };
22	};
23	
24	async function bootstrap(): Promise<NestFastifyApplication> {
25		const port = getEnv<number>({ key: PORT, converter: Number });
26		const globalPrefix = getEnv<string>({ key: GLOBAL_PREFIX });
27		const nodeEnv = getEnv<Environment>({ key: NODE_ENV });
28		const isDev = nodeEnv === Environment.DEVELOPMENT;
29	
30		const fastifyAdapter = new FastifyAdapter({ trustProxy: true });
31	
32		const app = await NestFactory.create<NestFastifyApplication>(
33			AppModule,
34			fastifyAdapter,
35			{
36				logger: isDev
37					? ['log', 'error', 'warn', 'fatal', 'debug', 'verbose']
38					: ['log', 'error', 'warn', 'fatal'],
39			},
40		);
41	
42		await app.register(fastifyCookie);
43	
44		app.enableCors({
45			origin: getEnv<string>({ key: FRONTEND_URL }),
46			credentials: true,
47		});
48	
49		app.useGlobalPipes(
50			new ValidationPipe({ transform: true, whitelist: true, forbidNonWhitelisted: true }),
51		);
52	
53		app.setGlobalPrefix(globalPrefix, {
54			exclude: ['liveness', 'readiness'],
55		});
56	
57		setupSwagger({ app, fastifyAdapter, globalPrefix });
58	
59		if (!isDev) {
60			app.enableShutdownHooks();
61		}
62	
63		await app.listen(port, '0.0.0.0');
64	
65		Logger.log(`Application running on port ${String(port)}`, 'Bootstrap');
66	
67		return app;
68	}
69	
70	let app: NestFastifyApplication | undefined;
71	
72	async function startApp(): Promise<void> {
73		try {
74			app = await bootstrap();
75		} catch (err: unknown) {
76			Logger.error('Failed to start application', err, 'Bootstrap');
77			process.exit(1);
78		}
79	}
80	
81	void startApp();
82	
83	if (module.hot) {
84		module.hot.accept();
85		module.hot.dispose(() => {
86			void app?.close();
87		});
88	}
89

> AGENT

I've read both codebases. The recommendations-service has:

1. **Custom logger** (`RecoConsoleLogger`) — wraps NestJS Logger, injects correlation ID into every log line, supports JSON/compact modes
2. **Correlation ID middleware** — extracts `x-correlation-id` from headers or generates UUID, uses `correlation-id-ts` library
3. **HTTP request logger** — Fastify plugin logging req/res with sensitive data masking
4. **Request store** — AsyncLocalStorage for request-scoped context (tied to Rollbar)
5. **LogExecution decorator** — already partially ported (yours lacks Rollbar, which is fine)

Your no-name-proj currently has: basic NestJS Logger, no correlation ID, no HTTP logging middleware, env vars for `IS_COMPACT_LOGS`/`IS_JSON_LIKE_LOGS`/`SERVER_NAME` already in place.

One question before I design this:

Do you want the **request store** (AsyncLocalStorage-based `requestStore` util) as well? In the recommendations-service it's mainly used for Rollbar context — which you don't have. I'd skip it unless you plan to add error tracking or need request-scoped storage for other purposes.

> DEVELOPER

@../recommendations-service/src/middlewares/correlation-id.middleware.ts @../recommendations-service/src/middlewares/logger.middleware.ts @../recommendations-service/src/configs/custom-logger.config.ts  reuse this

> AGENT

Clear. Here's the design for porting these 3 files + wiring:

### What gets created/modified

**New files:**
- `src/configs/custom-logger.config.ts` — `RecoConsoleLogger` as-is (rename to `AppConsoleLogger`?)
- `src/middlewares/correlation-id.middleware.ts` — as-is
- `src/middlewares/logger.middleware.ts` — as-is, uses existing `isDevEnv` from `@utils/common.util`
- `src/constants/req.constants.ts` — `CORRELATION_ID_HEADER_NAME` + `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`

**Modified files:**
- `main.ts` — use `AppConsoleLogger` as app logger, register `fastifyHttpLogger` plugin
- `app.module.ts` — apply `CorrelationIdMiddleware` for all routes
- `package.json` — add `correlation-id-ts`, `fastify-plugin`

**Adaptations from recommendations-service:**
- Strip Rollbar references (not in this project)
- Class name `RecoConsoleLogger` → `AppConsoleLogger` (or keep original?)
- `LogExecution` decorator already exists — no changes needed, it'll automatically get correlation IDs in logs via the custom logger

Two questions:
1. Rename `RecoConsoleLogger` → `AppConsoleLogger`, or keep original name?
2. Skip the request store (`AsyncLocalStorage`) — confirmed?

> DEVELOPER

1. rename
2. yep

?? 'stdout'].write(formattedMessage);\n\t\t});\n\t}\n\n\tprotected printAsJson(\n\t\tmessage: unknown,\n\t\tcorrelationId: string,\n\t\toptions: {\n\t\t\tcontext: string;\n\t\t\tlogLevel: LogLevel;\n\t\t\twriteStreamType?: 'stdout' | 'stderr';\n\t\t\terrorStack?: unknown;\n\t\t},\n\t) {\n\t\ttype JsonLogObject = {\n\t\t\tlevel: LogLevel;\n\t\t\tpid: number;\n\t\t\ttimestamp: number;\n\t\t\tcorrelationId: string;\n\t\t\tmessage: unknown;\n\t\t\tcontext?: string;\n\t\t\tstack?: unknown;\n\t\t};\n\n\t\tconst logObject: JsonLogObject = {\n\t\t\tlevel: options.logLevel,\n\t\t\tpid: process.pid,\n\t\t\ttimestamp: Date.now(),\n\t\t\tcorrelationId,\n\t\t\tmessage,\n\t\t};\n\n\t\tif (options.context) {\n\t\t\tlogObject.context = options.context;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (options.errorStack) {\n\t\t\tlogObject.stack = options.errorStack;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tconst formattedMessage =\n\t\t\t!this.options.colors && this.inspectOptions.compact === true\n\t\t\t\t? 'stdout'].write(`${formattedMessage}\\n`);\n\t}\n\n\tprotected formatPid(pid: number) {\n\t\treturn `[${this.options.prefix}] ${pid}  - `;\n\t}\n\n\tprotected formatContext(context: string): string outputText;\n\t}\n\n\tprotected colorize(message: string, logLevel: LogLevel) {\n\t\tif (!this.options.colors || this.options.json) {\n\t\t\treturn message;\n\t\t}\n\t\tconst color = this.getColorByLogLevel(logLevel);\n\t\treturn color(message);\n\t}\n\n\tprotected printStackTrace(stack: string) {\n\t\tif (!stack || this.options.json) {\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\t\tprocess.stderr.write(`${stack}\\n`);\n\t}\n\n\tprotected updateAndGetTimestampDiff(): string {\n\t\t// @ts-ignore\n\t\tconst includeTimestamp = ConsoleLogger.lastTimestampAt!)\n\t\t\t: '';\n\t\t// @ts-ignore\n\t\tConsoleLogger.lastTimestampAt = Date.now();\n\t\treturn result;\n\t}\n\n\tprotected formatTimestampDiff(timestampDiff: number) {\n\t\tconst formattedDiff = ` +${timestampDiff}ms`;\n\t\treturn this.options.colors ? yellow(formattedDiff) : formattedDiff;\n\t}\n\n\tprotected getInspectOptions() {\n\t\tlet breakLength = this.options.breakLength;\n\t\tif (typeof breakLength === 'undefined') {\n\t\t\tbreakLength = this.options.colors\n\t\t\t\t? this.options.compact\n\t\t\t\t\t? Infinity\n\t\t\t\t\t: undefined\n\t\t\t\t: this.options.compact === false\n\t\t\t\t\t? undefined\n\t\t\t\t\t: Infinity;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tconst inspectOptions: InspectOptions = {\n\t\t\tdepth: this.options.depth ?? DEFAULT_DEPTH,\n\t\t\tsorted: this.options.sorted,\n\t\t\tshowHidden: this.options.showHidden,\n\t\t\tcompact: this.options.compact ?? (this.options.json ? true : false),\n\t\t\tcolors: this.options.colors,\n\t\t\tbreakLength,\n\t\t};\n\n\t\tif (this.options.maxArrayLength) {\n\t\t\tinspectOptions.maxArrayLength = this.options.maxArrayLength;\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (this.options.maxStringLength) {\n\t\t\tinspectOptions.maxStringLength = this.options.maxStringLength;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn inspectOptions;\n\t}\n\n\tprotected stringifyReplacer(_: string, value: unknown) {\n\t\tif (typeof value === 'bigint') {\n\t\t\treturn value.toString();\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (typeof value === 'symbol') {\n\t\t\treturn value.toString();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (value instanceof Map || value instanceof Set || value instanceof Error) {\n\t\t\treturn `${inspect(value, this.inspectOptions)}`;\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn value;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate getContextAndMessagesToPrint(args: unknown[]) {\n\t\tif (args?.length <= 1) {\n\t\t\treturn { messages: args, context: this.context };\n\t\t}\n\t\tconst lastElement = args[args.length - 1];\n\t\tconst isContext = isString(lastElement);\n\t\tif (!isContext) {\n\t\t\treturn { messages: args, context: this.context };\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn {\n\t\t\tcontext: lastElement,\n\t\t\tmessages: args.slice(0, args.length - 1),\n\t\t};\n\t}\n\n\tprivate getContextAndStackAndMessagesToPrint(args: unknown[]) {\n\t\tif (args.length === 2) {\n\t\t\treturn this.isStackFormat(args[1])\n\t\t\t\t?   "content": "import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';\n\nimport { Injectable, NestMiddleware } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { correlator } from 'correlation-id-ts';\nimport { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n\nimport { CORRELATION_ID_HEADER_NAME } from '@constants/req.constants';\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class CorrelationIdMiddleware implements NestMiddleware {\n\tuse(req: FastifyRequest['raw'], _: FastifyReply['raw'], next: () => void): void {\n\t\tconst correlationIdFromHeader = req.headers[CORRELATION_ID_HEADER_NAME] as string;\n\n\t\tconst id = correlationIdFromHeader || randomUUID();\n\n\t\tcorrelator.withId({\n\t\t\tcorrelationId: id,\n\t\t\tfn: () => {\n\t\t\t\tnext();\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t});\n\t}\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { IncomingHttpHeaders } from 'node:http2';\n\nimport { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { FastifyRequest, FastifyReply, FastifyPluginCallback } from 'fastify';\nimport { HttpHeader } from 'fastify/types/utils';\nimport fp from 'fastify-plugin';\n\nimport { isDevEnv } from '@utils/common.util';\n\nconst FIELDS_WITH_SENSITIVE_DATA = [\n\t'req.body.password',\n\t'req.body.email',\n\t'req.body.tenant',\n\t'req.body.recipient',\n\t'req.headers.cookie',\n\t'req.headers.authorization',\n\t'req.headers.proxyAuthorization',\n\t'res.headers.cookie',\n\t'res.headers[\"set-cookie\"]',\n\t'res.headers.authorization',\n\t'res.headers.proxyAuthorization',\n];\n\nexport const logHttpRequest = (\n\treq: FastifyRequest,\n\tres: FastifyReply,\n\terror?: Error,\n): void => {\n\tconst logger = new Logger('HTTP Request');\n\tconst body = req.body ?? {};\n\tconst requestHeaders = req.headers;\n\tconst method = req.method;\n\tconst url = req.url;\n\tconst originalUrl = req.originalUrl;\n\tconst responseHeaders = res.getHeaders();\n\tconst statusCode = res.statusCode;\n\tconst ips = req.ips;\n\tconst ip = req.ip;\n\n\tconst validateHeaders = (\n\t\theaders:\n\t\t\t| IncomingHttpHeaders\n\t\t\t| Record<HttpHeader, string | number | string[] | undefined>,\n\t): Record<string, string> =>\n\t\tObject.entries(headers).reduce<Record<string, string>>((acc, [key, value]) => {\n\t\t\tif (FIELDS_WITH_SENSITIVE_DATA.includes(`req.headers.${key}`)) {\n\t\t\t\tacc[key] = isDevEnv() ? JSON.stringify(value) : '*****';\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tacc[key] = JSON.stringify(value);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn acc;\n\t\t}, {});\n\n\tconst validatedBody = Object.entries(body).reduce<Record<string, string>>(\n\t\t(acc, [key, value]) => {\n\t\t\tif (FIELDS_WITH_SENSITIVE_DATA.includes(`req.body.${key}`)) {\n\t\t\t\tacc[key] = isDevEnv() ? JSON.stringify(value) : '*****';\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tacc[key] = JSON.stringify(value);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn acc;\n\t\t},\n\t\t{},\n\t);\n\n\tconst isErrorStatus = statusCode >= 400;\n\tconst logMethod = isErrorStatus || error ? 'error' : 'log';\n\n\tconst logData: Record<string, unknown> = {\n\t\treq: {\n\t\t\tmethod,\n\t\t\turl,\n\t\t\toriginalUrl,\n\t\t\tips,\n\t\t\tip,\n\t\t\theaders: validateHeaders(requestHeaders),\n\t\t\tbody: validatedBody,\n\t\t},\n\t\tres: { statusCode, headers: validateHeaders(responseHeaders) },\n\t};\n\n\tif (error) {\n\t\tlogData['error'] = {\n\t\t\tmessage: error.message,\n\t\t\tname: error.name,\n\t\t\tstack: error.stack,\n\t\t};\n\t}\n\n\tlogger[logMethod](logData);\n};\n\nconst httpLoggerPlugin: FastifyPluginCallback = (fastify, _options, done) => {\n\tfastify.addHook('onResponse', (request, reply, done) => {\n\t\tif (reply.statusCode === 404) {\n\t\t\tconst notFoundError = new Error(\n\t\t\t\t`Route not found: ${request.method} ${request.url}`,\n\t\t\t);\n\t\t\tnotFoundError.name = 'NotFoundError';\n\t\t\tlogHttpRequest(request, reply, notFoundError);\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tlogHttpRequest(request, reply);\n\t\t}\n\t\tdone();\n\t});\n\n\tfastify.addHook('onError', (request, reply, error, done) => {\n\t\tlogHttpRequest(request, reply, error);\n\t\tdone();\n\t});\n\n\tdone();\n};\n\nexport const fastifyHttpLogger = fp(httpLoggerPlugin, {\n\tname: 'fastify-http-logger',\n});\n"
}

  "new_string": "import fastifyCookie from '@fastify/cookie';\nimport { Logger, ValidationPipe } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { FastifyAdapter, NestFastifyApplication } from '@nestjs/platform-fastify';\n\nimport { AppConsoleLogger } from '@config/custom-logger.config';\nimport { AppModule } from '@modules/app/app.module';\n\nimport { getEnv } from '@utils/get-env-variable.util';\nimport { setupSwagger } from '@utils/setup-swagger.util';\n\nimport {\n\tFRONTEND_URL,\n\tGLOBAL_PREFIX,\n\tIS_COMPACT_LOGS,\n\tIS_JSON_LIKE_LOGS,\n\tNODE_ENV,\n\tPORT,\n} from '@constants/env-variables.constant';\n\nimport { Environment } from '@interfaces/env-variables.interface';\n\nimport { fastifyHttpLogger } from '@middlewares/logger.middleware';\n\ndeclare const module: {\n\thot?: { accept: () => void; dispose: (callback: () => void) => void };\n};\n\nasync function bootstrap(): Promise<NestFastifyApplication> {\n\tconst port = getEnv<number>({ key: PORT, converter: Number });\n\tconst globalPrefix = getEnv<string>({ key: GLOBAL_PREFIX });\n\tconst nodeEnv = getEnv<Environment>({ key: NODE_ENV });\n\tconst isDev = nodeEnv === Environment.DEVELOPMENT;\n\tconst isCompactLogs = getEnv<string>({ key: IS_COMPACT_LOGS }) === 'true';\n\tconst isJsonLikeLogs = getEnv<string>({ key: IS_JSON_LIKE_LOGS }) === 'true';\n\n\tconst logger = new AppConsoleLogger({\n\t\tlogLevels: isDev\n\t\t\t? ['log', 'error', 'warn', 'fatal', 'debug', 'verbose']\n\t\t\t: ['log', 'error', 'warn', 'fatal'],\n\t\tcompact: isCompactLogs,\n\t\tjson: isJsonLikeLogs,\n\t\tcolors: !isJsonLikeLogs,\n\t});\n\n\tconst fastifyAdapter = new FastifyAdapter({ trustProxy: true });\n\n\tawait fastifyAdapter.getInstance().register(fastifyHttpLogger);\n\n\tconst app = await NestFactory.create<NestFastifyApplication>(\n\t\tAppModule,\n\t\tfastifyAdapter,\n\t\t{ logger },\n\t);"
}

  "new_string": "import { MiddlewareConsumer, Module, NestModule } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { APP_GUARD } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { ThrottlerModule } from '@nestjs/throttler';\n\nimport { AuthModule } from '@modules/auth/auth.module';\nimport { AuthGuard } from '@modules/auth/guards/auth.guard';\nimport { FastifyThrottlerGuard } from '@modules/auth/guards/fastify-throttler.guard';\nimport { RolesGuard } from '@modules/auth/guards/roles.guard';\nimport { CacheModule } from '@modules/cache/cache.module';\nimport { CompanyModule } from '@modules/company/company.module';\nimport { CompanyMemberModule } from '@modules/company-member/company-member.module';\nimport { DbModule } from '@modules/db/db.module';\nimport { EnvConfigModule } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.module';\nimport { HealthModule } from '@modules/health/health.module';\nimport { InvitationModule } from '@modules/invitation/invitation.module';\nimport { SessionModule } from '@modules/session/session.module';\nimport { UserModule } from '@modules/user/user.module';\n\nimport { CorrelationIdMiddleware } from '@middlewares/correlation-id.middleware';\n\n@Module({\n\timports: [\n\t\tEnvConfigModule,\n\t\tHealthModule,\n\t\tDbModule,\n\t\tCacheModule,\n\t\tThrottlerModule.forRoot([{ ttl: 60000, limit: 60 }]),\n\t\tAuthModule,\n\t\tUserModule,\n\t\tCompanyModule,\n\t\tCompanyMemberModule,\n\t\tInvitationModule,\n\t\tSessionModule,\n\t],\n\tcontrollers: [],\n\tproviders: [\n\t\t{ provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: AuthGuard },\n\t\t{ provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: RolesGuard },\n\t\t{ provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: FastifyThrottlerGuard },\n\t],\n})\nexport class AppModule implements NestModule {\n\tconfigure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer): void {\n\t\tconsumer.apply(CorrelationIdMiddleware).forRoutes('*path');\n\t}\n}"
}

Done. Here's what was added:

**New files:**
- `src/configs/custom-logger.config.ts` — `AppConsoleLogger` with correlation ID in every log line
- `src/middlewares/correlation-id.middleware.ts` — extracts/generates `x-correlation-id` per request
- `src/middlewares/logger.middleware.ts` — Fastify plugin logging all HTTP req/res with sensitive data masking
- `src/constants/req.constants.ts` — `CORRELATION_ID_HEADER_NAME`, `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`

**Modified files:**
- `main.ts` — uses `AppConsoleLogger` with `IS_COMPACT_LOGS`/`IS_JSON_LIKE_LOGS` config, registers `fastifyHttpLogger` plugin
- `app.module.ts` — implements `NestModule`, applies `CorrelationIdMiddleware` to all routes

**New dependency:** `correlation-id-ts`

6	import { RecoConsoleLogger } from '@config/custom-logger.config';
7	import { rollbar } from '@config/rollbar.config';
8	
9	import { AppModule } from '@modules/app/app.module';
10	import { NewrelicService } from '@modules/newrelic/newrelic.service';
11	
12	import { getEnv } from '@utils/get-env-variable.util';
13	import { setupSwagger } from '@utils/setup-swagger.util';
14	
15	import { BadRequestExceptionFilter } from '@middlewares/bad-request-exception.filter';
16	import { CorrelationIdMiddleware } from '@middlewares/correlation-id.middleware';
17	import { RollbarExceptionFilter } from '@middlewares/exceptions.filter';
18	import { fastifyHttpLogger } from '@middlewares/logger.middleware';
19	
20	import {
21		NODE_ENV,
22		PORT,
23		SERVER_NAME,
24		GLOBAL_PREFIX,
25		IS_COMPACT_LOGS,
26		IS_JSON_LIKE_LOGS,
27	} from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
28	
29	import { Environment } from '@interfaces/env-variables.interface';
30	
31	import { NewrelicInterceptor } from '@interceptors/newrelic.interceptor';
32	
33	declare const module: {
34		hot?: { accept: () => void; dispose: (callback: () => void) => void };
35	};
36	
37	async function bootstrap(): Promise<NestFastifyApplication> {
38		const globalPrefix = getEnv<string>({ key: GLOBAL_PREFIX });
39		const port = getEnv<number>({ key: PORT, converter: Number });
40		const nodeEnv = getEnv<Environment>({ key: NODE_ENV });
41		const serverName = getEnv<string>({ key: SERVER_NAME });
42		const isCompactLogs = getEnv<boolean>({ key: IS_COMPACT_LOGS, converter: Boolean });
43		const isJsonLikeLogs = getEnv<boolean>({ key: IS_JSON_LIKE_LOGS, converter: Boolean });
44		const isDev = nodeEnv === Environment.DEVELOPMENT;
45		const isProdOrStaging =
46			nodeEnv === Environment.PRODUCTION || nodeEnv === Environment.STAGING;
47	
48		const logger = new RecoConsoleLogger({
49			logLevels: isDev
50				? ['log', 'error', 'warn', 'fatal', 'debug', 'verbose']
51				: ['log', 'error', 'warn', 'fatal'],
52			colors: isDev,
53			prefix: serverName,
54			compact: isCompactLogs,
55			json: isJsonLikeLogs,
56		});
57	
58		const fastifyAdapter = new FastifyAdapter({
59			trustProxy: true,
60		});
61		fastifyAdapter.getInstance().register(fastifyHttpLogger);
62	
63		const app = await NestFactory.create<NestFastifyApplication>(
64			AppModule,
65			fastifyAdapter,
66			{ bodyParser: false, rawBody: true, logger },
67		);
68	
69		if (nodeEnv !== Environment.PRODUCTION) {
70			setupSwagger(app, fastifyAdapter, globalPrefix);
71		}
72	
73		app.enableCors({ origin: true, credentials: true });
74	
75		app.use((req: FastifyRequest['raw'], res: FastifyReply['raw'], next: () => void) => {
76			const middleware = new CorrelationIdMiddleware();
77			middleware.use(req, res, next);
78		});
79	
80		if (isProdOrStaging) {
81			app.useGlobalInterceptors(
82				new NewrelicInterceptor(await app.resolve<NewrelicService>(NewrelicService)),
83			);
84			app.useGlobalFilters(new RollbarExceptionFilter());
85		}
86	
87		app.useGlobalFilters(new BadRequestExceptionFilter());
88	
89		app.useGlobalPipes(
90			new ValidationPipe({ transform: true, whitelist: true, forbidNonWhitelisted: true }),
91		);
92	
93		app.setGlobalPrefix(globalPrefix, {
94			exclude: ['is_it_up', 'is_it_working', 'is_it_ready', 'robots.txt'],
95		});
96	
97		if (!isDev) {
98			app.enableShutdownHooks();
99		}
100	
101		logger.log(`[Main file] Application is running on ${String(port)} port`);
102		await app.listen(port);
103	
104		if (!isDev) {
105			const LogShutDown = (signal: string): void => {
106				logger.log(`Received ${signal} server is shutting down`);
107			};
108			process.on('SIGINT', LogShutDown);
109			process.on('SIGTERM', LogShutDown);
110		}
111	
112		return app;
113	}
114	
115	let app: NestFastifyApplication | undefined;
116	
117	async function startApp(): Promise<void> {
118		try {
119			app = await bootstrap();
120		} catch (err: unknown) {
121			Logger.flush();
122			rollbar.critical(err as Error);
123			rollbar.wait(() => process.exit(1));
124		}
125	}
126	
127	void startApp();
128	
129	if (module.hot) {
130		module.hot.accept();
131		module.hot.dispose(() => {
132			void app?.close();
133		});
134	}
135	
```

All 7 tests pass with 100% coverage on all metrics (statements, branches, functions, lines).

---

**File created:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/backend/src/unit-tests/correlation-id.middleware.unit-spec.ts`

Key decisions made:

**Mocking approach.** `jest.mock` factories are hoisted before any variable declarations, so the `mockCorrelator` object could not be referenced directly inside the factory. The solution is to define the mock object inline inside the factory, then import the mocked module after the `jest.mock` calls and cast to `jest.Mocked<T>` to get typed mock methods.

**UUID type constraint.** `randomUUID` returns a `${string}-${string}-${string}-${string}-${string}` template literal type. Using an arbitrary string like `'generated-uuid-456'` caused a TypeScript error under the strict test tsconfig, so a properly formatted UUID is used as the mock return value.

**Test cases covered:**
1. Header present — uses the header value as `correlationId`, does not call `randomUUID`
2. Header absent — calls `randomUUID` and passes the generated value as `correlationId`
3. `correlator.withId` called with correct shape (`correlationId` + `fn`)
4. `next()` invoked when `withId` calls `fn` (default behavior)
5. `next()` invoked when `withId` explicitly calls `fn` (explicit re-mock)
6. `next()` not invoked when `withId` does not call `fn` (verifying `next` lives inside `fn`, not outside it)
7. Injectable (`should be defined` via `Test.createTestingModule`)

The test file is solid. Here is a summary of what was created:

---

**File created:** `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/backend/src/unit-tests/logger.middleware.unit-spec.ts`

**42 tests across two top-level describe blocks:**

`logHttpRequest`:
- **Logging method selection** (5 tests): verifies `Logger.log` is used for status < 400 with no error, `Logger.error` for status >= 400, and `Logger.error` when an error is passed regardless of status code.
- **Request data logging** (7 tests): verifies method, url, originalUrl, ips, ip, statusCode are present in the log payload; that `getHeaders()` is called; that `undefined` body falls back to `{}`; and that non-sensitive fields are JSON-stringified.
- **Sensitive data masking in non-dev env** (10 tests): one test per sensitive field (`password`, `email`, `tenant`, `recipient` in body; `cookie`, `authorization`, `proxyAuthorization` in request headers; `cookie`, `authorization`, `proxyAuthorization` in response headers) confirming `*****` masking, plus a combined multi-field test.
- **Sensitive data shown in dev env** (5 tests): same fields confirmed to show their JSON-stringified actual values when `isDevEnv()` returns `true`.
- **Error details** (3 tests): verifies `error.message`, `error.name`, `error.stack` are included in the log payload when an error is provided, and that the `error` key is absent when no error is passed.

`fastifyHttpLogger`:
- **Plugin registration** (2 tests): confirms both `onResponse` and `onError` hooks are registered via `addHook`.
- **onResponse hook** (6 tests): non-404 logs normally; 404 creates a `NotFoundError` with the correct message and logs as error; 500 logs as error; 201 logs normally; `done()` is always called.
- **onError hook** (3 tests): `done()` is called; error message, name, and stack are included in the log payload.

The approach for invoking the `fp`-wrapped plugin is to cast `fastifyHttpLogger` directly to the underlying callback signature `(fastify, options, done) => void`, then call it with a mock Fastify instance that captures `addHook` registrations for later invocation — avoiding any need to mock `fastify-plugin` itself.

242		private shouldReadFromMaster({
243			key,
244			consistencyLevel,
245		}: {
246			namespace: Namespaces;
247			key: string;
248			consistencyLevel: ConsistencyLevel;
249		}): boolean {
250			if (consistencyLevel === ConsistencyLevel.STRONG) {
251				return true;
252			}
253	
254			if (!this.isReadReplicaHealthy) {
255				return true;
256			}
257	
258			const lastWriteTime = this.recentWrites.get(key);
259			if (lastWriteTime) {
260				const timeSinceWrite = Date.now() - lastWriteTime;
261				return timeSinceWrite < this.CONSISTENCY_WINDOW_MS;
262			}
263	
264			return false;
265		}
266	
267		private trackRecentWrite({ key }: { key: string }): void {
268			this.recentWrites.set(key, Date.now());
269		}
270	
271		private startRecentWritesCleanup(): void {
272			setInterval(() => {
273				const now = Date.now();
274				for (const [key, timestamp] of this.recentWrites.entries()) {
275					if (now - timestamp > this.CONSISTENCY_WINDOW_MS * 2) {
276						this.recentWrites.delete(key);
277					}
278				}
279			}, this.CONSISTENCY_WINDOW_MS).unref();
280		}
281	
282		private setupHealthChecks(): void {

All 592 tests pass across 35 suites with full coverage above the 95% threshold.

The test file is at `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/backend/src/unit-tests/log-execution.decorator.unit-spec.ts`.

Here is a summary of what was written and the results:

**41 tests in 2 top-level describe blocks**

`maskSensitiveData` (22 tests):
- Primitives and nullish values — null, undefined, string, number, boolean returned unchanged
- Flat objects — each default sensitive key masked individually (password, token, secret, auth, authorization, cookie, apiKey, refreshToken, accessToken), case-insensitive substring matching, non-sensitive keys untouched
- Nested objects — recursive masking on nested plain objects; masking the parent key when it matches (e.g. `credentials`)
- Arrays — each element processed, primitive array elements returned unchanged
- Custom `sensitiveKeys` — only the provided keys are masked
- Inherited properties — the `hasOwnProperty` guard is exercised via `Object.create(proto)` to ensure inherited enumerable properties are skipped

`LogExecution` (19 tests):
- Default options — params masked, response masked, both log entries emitted, result returned correctly
- `logParams: false` — Invoked entry absent, Completed entry present
- `logResponse: false` — `"response"` field absent from completion log
- `logExecutionTime: false` — Completed entry absent entirely
- `maskSensitiveData: false` — raw params and response logged verbatim
- `paramFormatter` — formatter output used instead of raw args
- `responseFormatter` — formatter output used instead of raw response
- `customContext` — extra fields appear in every log entry
- `sensitiveKeys` — custom keys masked in both param and response logs
- Slow execution warning — `performance.now` mocked to simulate 4000ms, `Logger.warn` called with correct message including class and method name
- Fast execution — no warn emitted
- Error handling — `Logger.error` called with message and stack, error re-thrown
- Request object detection — Fastify-like arg replaced with `{method, url, ip, userAgent}`; empty object not treated as request; object with insufficient keys not treated as request
- Response object detection — arg with `send`+`status` functions replaced with `[FastifyReply]`; null arg and string arg pass through unchanged
- `className` and `methodName` — correct names appear in both log entries

**Coverage achieved on `log-execution.decorator.ts`:** 98.55% statements, 98.07% branches, 100% functions, 100% lines. The single uncovered branch is the `hasOwnProperty` guard's `continue` path (line 95), which requires `Object.prototype` pollution to trigger and is a purely defensive safety net.
